### WP-13 E3.2: BayCAP-KDEy with a random $C$ (joint posterior of $(\pi,C)$, kernel likelihood, Hf prior), on the `main` protocol

- **Kind / status:** experiment / Done (2026-11-09)
- **Source:** [[ext3_posterior_propagation]] E3.2 (§T.10–§T.13)
- **Goal:** the realised BayCAP-KDEy with a fixed $\hat C$ (E3.1, §II.4) under-covers where the error comes from $V$. Make $C$ random: split each class of $V$ into cells by the prediction of $h$, take one Gaussian kernel density ($b=0.1$, no shrinkage) per cell, model the class density as $f_j=\sum_kC_{kj}\hat\phi_{kj}$, and sample $(\pi,C)$ jointly by NUTS ($500$ warm-up iterations, $1000$ draws, the seed of `BayesCAP`, $\tau=1$) with $\pi\sim\operatorname{Dir}(\mathbf 1)$, the Hf prior on $C$ (centred shares, as `BayesCAP-Hf-real`, §II.18) and the validation counts observed as multinomials, as in `BayCAP`. Likelihood of the bag: for the input classifier `h`, $\sum_u\log\sum_j\pi_j\sum_kC_{kj}\hat\phi_{kj}(z_u)$; for `mlp` (the MLP of E3.1, cross-fitted on $V$), $\sum_u\log\sum_j\pi_jC_{\hat y_uj}\hat\phi_{\hat y_uj}(z_u)$, with $\hat y_u$ the prediction of $h$. An empty cell $(k,j)$ borrows the kernel density of all validation instances predicted $\omega_k$, or that of class $\omega_j$ if $h$ never predicts $\omega_k$ on $V$. Realised pushforward. Compare with `BayCAP-KDEy-<s>-real` (E3.1) and `BayesCAP-Hf-real`: AE, coverage, amplitude and Winkler score for the three metrics; for accuracy, the split of the shift into $R$ and $P$, paired bootstrap Winkler tests against both references (Holm over the $75$ pairs), the standardised shift, NUTS diagnostics and Hf summaries, and, label-free, the ratio of the posterior sd of each $C_{kj}$ to its sd from $V$ alone, the mismatch $\lVert n^t/N^Q-\bar C\bar\pi\rVert_1$, and the numbers of empty and borrowed cells. No decision rule (user's decision of 2026-11-06).
- **Estimand:** realised table of the target sample, scored against `true_cts`.
- **Scope:** split `methods/baycap_fixed.py:BayCAPFixed` into `methods/baycap_kdey.py:BayCAPKDEy` and `methods/baycap_mapls.py:BayCAPMAPLS` (shared code in a common module), with `c_prior` $\in\{$`"fixed"`, `"hierarchical-f"`$\}$ on `BayCAPKDEy`; two factory entries `BayCAP-KDEy-Hf-h-real` and `BayCAP-KDEy-Hf-mlp-real` in `experiments/methods.py` (BayCAP pool, `BAYES_ENV`, excluded from the tables, ranks and default plots of `main`); per-bag storage of the NUTS diagnostics, the Hf summaries, the posterior mean and sd of $C$, the mismatch and the numbers of empty and borrowed cells; a table module and a plot module for E3.2 (comparison table with the paired Winkler tests and the standardised shift, information on $C$ per cell, diagnostics); `tables/rank.py` and `tables/displacement.py --method` for the new methods. Pilot on `mammographic`, `poker_hand` and `wine-quality`.
- **Depends on:** none (uses the Hf helpers of WP-11 and the realised pushforward of WP-4).
- **Comparability:** the split must reproduce the draws of `BayCAPFixed` in the eight configurations of E3.1 (tested), so the stored E3.1 results stay valid; adds the rows of two methods to `main` and changes nothing else. §II.4 and §II.12 are updated for the split.
- **Deviations from Part V:** a NumPyro model of its own, not a copy of QuaPy's, so for `c_prior="hierarchical-f"` there is no equality test with QuaPy's `sample_from_posterior`; the check is instead the reduction to BayesKDEy at $C=\hat C$ for `h`. The code shared by `BayCAPKDEy` and `BayCAPMAPLS` is a base class, `methods/baycap_quantifier.py:QuantifierBayCAP`, not plain functions (§V.2, "Helpers shared by several methods are plain functions"): `fit`, the NUTS run, the pushforward of a fixed $\hat C$ and the diagnostics then exist once, as they did in `BayCAPFixed`, which keeps the split draw-for-draw identical and lets `main` recognise both classes by one `isinstance` check. The equality with `BayCAPFixed` is tested against a frozen copy of it in `tests/_reference_baycap_fixed.py` (commit `55247a1`), since the class is removed.
- **Done when:** the full run has stored results for both methods on the $75$ pairs, the tables and plots are generated, and the Part II section records the result location, the `cap-ml` commit and the date.
- **Commands:** all on `bcuda` (the default), each in its own tmux session; `make logs EXP=<target>` shows the output, `make explog EXP=main` the experiment log. The two methods are `BayCAP-KDEy-Hf-h-real` and `BayCAP-KDEy-Hf-mlp-real`; they run in the BayCAP pool, on CPU (`BAYES_ENV`).
  - *Pilot:* `mammographic`, `poker_hand` and `wine-quality` with the five classifiers, both methods: `make run-main-classic ARGS="--methods BayCAP-KDEy-Hf-h-real BayCAP-KDEy-Hf-mlp-real --datasets mammographic poker_hand wine-quality"` ($15$ pairs × $2$ methods × $100$ bags $=3\,000$ NUTS runs); the times per bag are in `t_test_ave`.
  - *Full:* one method per run, one run at a time (each uses the whole BayCAP pool): `make run-main-classic ARGS="--methods <method>"` for each of the two ($75\times100=7\,500$ NUTS runs each); cells already stored, e.g. by the pilot, are skipped.
  - *Tables:* `make run-tables-baycap-kdey-hf ARGS=--tables` (needs the stored results of `BayCAP-KDEy-h-real`, `BayCAP-KDEy-mlp-real`, `BayesCAP-Hf-real` and `RQBS`; it works on the pairs stored for all six methods, so also after the pilot), then `make pull` and `make run-tables-baycap-kdey-hf WHERE=local ARGS=--pdf` where `pdflatex` is installed. Decomposition of E2.2: `make run-tables-displacement ARGS="--tables --method <method>"` for each of the two (prior part $R_0$ reported as NaN). Rank table with the two: `make run-tables-rank ARGS="--tables --include-e32"` (output under `<OUT_DIR>/uncawe/tables/rank/extended/`; with `--include-e31` too, it also ranks the eight methods of E3.1).
  - *Plots:* `make run-plot-baycap-kdey-hf` (after the tables; output under `<OUT_DIR>/uncawe/plots/baycap_kdey_hf/`).
- **Log:** 2026-11-06: card added by the user. 2026-11-06: started on branch `wp-13/baycap-kdey-hf`. Split of `BayCAPFixed` into `BayCAPKDEy` and `BayCAPMAPLS` on `QuantifierBayCAP`, with the same draws in the eight E3.1 configurations (`tests/test_baycap_split.py`); `BayCAPFixed` removed. The Hf sites of `BayCAP._hierarchical_model` moved to the function `methods/baycap.py:hierarchical_rate_sites` (same sites, same order, so the draws of `BayCAP` are unchanged), shared with `BayCAPKDEy`. `BayCAPKDEy` with `c_prior="hierarchical-f"` (`kdey_hf_model`, `kdey_hf_loglik`, cells and empty cells of §T.12, per-bag summaries `KDEY_HF_SUMMARIES`); factory entries `BayCAP-KDEy-Hf-h-real` and `BayCAP-KDEy-Hf-mlp-real` (`KDEY_HF_VARIANTS`), excluded from the tables, ranks and default plots of `main` (then, at the user's request, added to the tables, ranks and coverage vs amplitude plot of `main`), with labels and colours; `tables/rank.py --include-e32`; `tables/displacement.py --method` accepts them, with a prior part of NaN for the hierarchical priors (before, `--method BayesCAP-Hf-real` raised an error in `smoothed_recalls`). Stored per bag: the empty cells are counted as `n_empty_cells`, and those that take the class density as `n_class_density_cells` (the others take the density of the predicted class). Table module `tables/baycap_kdey_hf.py` and plot module `plots/baycap_kdey_hf.py` (tests: `tests/test_baycap_kdey_hf_tables.py`), `make` targets `tables-baycap-kdey-hf` and `plot-baycap-kdey-hf`. Deviation from the planned representation: the comparison table of E3.2 is split in two LaTeX tables, the scores with the standardised shift (`comparison`) and the four paired tests with their intervals and Holm-adjusted p-values (`tests`), which do not fit one page together; the scatter of the information on $C$ leaves out the cells with no ratio ($f_{kj}=0$, or $\hat C_{kj}\in\{0,1\}$, where the standard deviation from $V$ alone is $0$), counted in its title. The split of the shift into $R$ and $P$ is also in the per-pair CSV of E3.2. Code and unit tests done (254 tests pass). 2026-11-06: at the user's request, the two methods were added to the tables, ranks and coverage vs amplitude plot of `main` (`b9fe044`); pilot started on `bcuda` at 18:54 from `b9fe044` (`JAX=cpu`, `mammographic`, `poker_hand`, `wine-quality`, five classifiers, both methods; log `output/runs/main-classic_20261106-185412.out`). 2026-11-06: pilot done at 20:18 (exit status $0$, 30 of 30 tasks stored, no errors in the logs). Mean time per bag: about $3$–$4$ s on `mammographic`; on `wine-quality` $14$–$33$ s for `h` and $5$–$8$ s for `mlp`; on `poker_hand` $32$–$39$ s for `h` and $12$–$14$ s for `mlp` (`h` evaluates all $K^2$ cells per instance, `mlp` only $K$), plus $33$–$37$ s of MLP training per pair on `poker_hand`. The share of failed or divergent bags was not checked (no analysis script run). 2026-11-06: at the user's request, the comparison table of E3.2 shows the scores of E3.1 in its order (AE, amplitude, coverage, Winkler score, then the standardised shift; before, AE was only in the CSV) and sets the method labels vertically, as in the tables of `main`. 2026-11-06: at the user's request, the E3.2 tables were generated on `bcuda` from the pilot results (`7b53cf9`, 15 pairs, exit status $0$) and their PDFs built locally; no failed bags, divergent transitions in some bag of every pair, largest $\hat R$ high on 5 pairs (`h`) and 3 (`mlp`). 2026-11-06: full run started at the user's request (kept as a reference, the user being not convinced by the methods): `BayCAP-KDEy-Hf-mlp-real` on `bcuda` at 21:52 from `70b2140` (log `output/runs/main-classic_20261106-215250.out`), then `BayCAP-KDEy-Hf-h-real` when it ends; the pilot cells are skipped. 2026-11-06: `BayCAP-KDEy-Hf-mlp-real` done at 23:55 (exit status $0$, 60 new tasks, the 15 pilot pairs skipped, no errors in the logs); `BayCAP-KDEy-Hf-h-real` started at 23:58 from `847e508` (log `output/runs/main-classic_20261106-235817.out`). 2026-11-07: `BayCAP-KDEy-Hf-h-real` done at 20:57 (exit status $0$, 60 new tasks, no errors in the logs); the slowest pairs are those of `isolet` ($K=26$), $600$–$755$ s per bag, because `h` evaluates the $K^2$ cell densities of every target instance. Both methods are now stored for all 75 pairs; tables and plots of the full run not generated yet. 2026-11-09: at the user's request, on `bcuda` from `a215a14`: the tables of `main` (`make run-tables-main ARGS=--tables`), the E3.2 tables on the 75 pairs and the three E3.2 plots (exit status $0$ for each); PDFs built locally. No failed bags; divergent transitions in some bag of 59 (`h`) and 58 (`mlp`) pairs, largest $\hat R$ high on 18 and 15 pairs, mostly with $K\ge8$. 2026-11-09: E3.2 analysed (the Analysis of E3.2 in [[ext3_posterior_propagation]], from the per-pair and per-cell CSVs of E3.2 and the tables of `main`; the ranks among six methods, the head-to-head counts and the sign and Wilcoxon tests computed from the per-pair CSV, outside the analysis script); by the user's decision, the two methods are not carried forward, `BayCAP-KDEy-mlp-real` stays the BayCAP-KDEy method and `BayesCAP-Hf-real` the reference for coverage and mean Winkler score; a blend of the two is candidate E3.c8. Card closed at the user's request.
- **Outcome:** merged into `devel` on 2026-11-09 (`836f990`), at the user's request, without a rebase. `devel` had one new commit, `97101cb` (the user's eight-method sets of the tables, ranks and coverage vs amplitude plot of `main`), which conflicted with the branch in `tables/main.py`, `tables/rank.py`, `plots/amplitude_vs_coverage.py`, `tests/test_methods.py` and §V.4 of this document; the conflicts were resolved in favour of `devel`, so the two methods of E3.2, shown in those tables and in the plot on the branch at the user's request, are excluded after the merge (`tables/rank.py --include-e32` still adds them to the extended rank table), and `tests/test_methods.py` also checks their exclusion. Deviations from the card are in its `Deviations` field (an own NumPyro model, checked by its reduction to BayesKDEy; the base class `QuantifierBayCAP`). Decisions beyond the card, in the log: the two methods in the tables of `main` on the branch; the comparison table of E3.2 split in two (scores, tests). Problems: divergent transitions in some bag of $58$–$59$ pairs, many with $K\ge8$ (about $430$ per bag on `poker_hand`), with the posterior of $\kappa$ below $1$ on $47$ of the $50$ pairs with $K\ge3$; slow runs with `h` at large $K$ ($600$–$755$ s per bag on `isolet`); the rank table with `--include-e32` and the decompositions of E2.2 for the two methods were not generated. Result: no improvement over E3.1 overall; by the user's decision of 2026-11-09 the methods are not carried forward (§II.4). Unit tests: 254, all passing at the merge. Follow-ups: candidate E3.c8 (a blend of BayCAP-KDEy and BayCAP-Hf) of [[ext3_posterior_propagation]]; the hypotheses of the Analysis of E3.2 (the fixed cell densities identify $\pi$; the bag treats the cell densities as exact, testable with $\tau>1$ or smaller bags); the unexplained failures on `shuttle` (`mlp`) and `german` (`h`).

### WP-8 E3.1: BayCAP with a fixed $\hat C$, quantifier × input classifier × pushforward, on the `main` protocol

- **Kind / status:** experiment / Done (2026-11-05)
- **Source:** [[ext3_posterior_propagation]] (E3.1, §T.3–§T.9)
- **Goal:** evaluate CAP methods that push the joint posterior draws $\pi^{(r)}$ of a Bayesian quantifier through the rate matrix of $h$ measured on $V$, $\hat C\operatorname{diag}(\pi^{(r)})$ with $\hat C_{ij}=f_{ij}/n^s_j$ (no prior, not sampled), and separate the effect of three components. Background: with a fixed $\hat C$, accuracy is $\hat d^\top\pi$, so the population interval has variance $(\hat d-\bar d\mathbf 1)^\top\Sigma_\pi(\hat d-\bar d\mathbf 1)$ and is narrow when the recalls are similar; it leaves out the luck of the bag; the realised pushforward should widen it by about $\sqrt{1+(\bar\sigma_g/\sigma_p)^2}$ and may move its centre, because the quantifiers fit $\pi$ to the outputs of an input classifier, not to the predictions of $h$.
  - *Quantifier:* `KDEy`, QuaPy's `BayesianKDEy` with `kernel="gaussian"`, `bandwidth=0.1`, `shrinkage=0.0` (fixed, not selected); or `EMQ`, QuaPy's `BayesianMAPLS` with its defaults (`prior="uniform"`, `exact_train_prev=True`, `mapls_chain_init=True`). Its NUTS model samples ILR coordinates from $\mathcal N(\mathbf 0,I)$, so its effective prior on $\pi$ is logistic-normal, not uniform; kept as in QuaPy (user's decision of 2026-10-28).
  - *Input classifier:* `h`, the posteriors of $h$ on $V$ (out of sample, since $h$ was trained on $L$) and on $U$, given to the quantifier's `aggregation_fit` and to the posterior sampling without training a classifier; or `mlp`, a separate `MLPClassifier()` with default settings and a seeded `random_state`, trained on $V$ with QuaPy's 5-fold cross-fitted validation outputs.
  - *Pushforward:* `population`, the tables $\hat C\operatorname{diag}(\pi^{(r)})$; or `realised`, `methods/baycap.py:realised_tables` applied to them with $n^t$ from the arg-max of the posteriors of $h$ on $U$.
  - *Methods:* eight, `BayCAP-<Q>-<s>` and `BayCAP-<Q>-<s>-real` for $Q\in\{$`KDEy`, `EMQ`$\}$ and $s\in\{$`h`, `mlp`$\}$, each in its own run of `main`. Held fixed: $\tau=1$ (`temperature=1`), `num_warmup=BAYES_WARMUPS`, `num_samples=BOOTSTRAP_ITERS`, the default seed of `BayesCAP`; the population and realised methods of a cell share their prevalence draws.
  - *Check before coding:* with `EMQ` and `h`, QuaPy sets the source prevalence $\pi^P$ to the class proportions of $V$, while the outputs of $h$ refer to those of $L$. Check whether $V$ is a stratified split of the same data as $L$ (`pretrain/`), and report $\max_j\lvert\pi^L_j-\pi^V_j\rvert$ per dataset; if it is not small, the user decides whether to keep QuaPy's behaviour.
  - *Measures* (vanilla accuracy unless stated): for all eight methods and the three metrics of `main`, AE, coverage, amplitude, Winkler score; $\mathcal C_s$ and the split of the shift into $R$ and $P$ with $R_0=0$; for the realised methods, the observed widening factor (amplitude realised / population) against $\sqrt{1+(\bar\sigma_g/\sigma_p)^2}$, and the movement of the centre (realised minus population point estimate); the label-free mismatch $\lVert n^t/N^Q-\hat C\bar\pi\rVert_1$; $\operatorname{sd}_j(\hat C_{jj})$; the top-label ECE (15 equal-width bins) of each input classifier on the validation outputs the quantifier is fitted on; coverage in bins of $\min_j\pi^Q_j$; the NUTS divergences, split-$\hat R$ and effective sample size of $\pi$; paired bootstrap tests of the mean Winkler difference as in E2.3 (10,000 seeded resamples, Holm over the 75 pairs, per test): realised vs population, vs `BayesCAP-real` and vs `BayesCAP-Hf-real` in each cell (12), `h` vs `mlp` per quantifier (2), `KDEy` vs `EMQ` per input classifier (2), all on the realised methods except the first.
  - *Tables and plots:* realised and population comparison tables (one row per pair, by dataset and $K$, with `BayesCAP(-real)`, `RQBS` and, in the realised one, `BayesCAP-Hf-real`, CSV/LaTeX/landscape PDF, as `tables/pushforward.py`); a 2×2 summary per pushforward and metric (mean Winkler rank, coverage, amplitude; overall and per classifier); a rank table of all methods of `main` with the eight; the E2.2 decomposition via `tables/displacement.py --method`; scatters of coverage (vs `BayesCAP-real` / `BayesCAP` / `BayesCAP-Hf-real`), width (observed vs predicted factor, coloured by mismatch), centre (movement vs mismatch), calibration (coverage vs ECE; coverage vs bins of $\min_j\pi^Q_j$), one panel per cell.
- **Estimand:** population table for the four methods without `-real`, realised table for the four with `-real`; all scored against the realised table of each bag (`true_cts`). The split of the shift and the bins of $\min_j\pi^Q_j$ use target labels, so they are analyses; $n^t$, $\bar\pi$ and the ECE use none.
- **Scope:**
  - `methods/`: one class for the eight methods, generalising `baycap_kdey.py:BayCAPKDEy` with `quantifier`, `input_classifier`, `estimand` and a `random_state` that seeds the MLP, NUTS and the multinomial draws (§V.2), and with `last_nuts_diagnostics` (divergences, split-$\hat R$ and ESS of $\pi$, as for `BayCAP`). The class runs NUTS itself, with `extra_fields=("diverging",)`, on QuaPy's `_numpyro_model` of each quantifier after the same preparation as QuaPy's `sample_from_posterior`; a model is reimplemented only if it cannot be reused (user's agreement of 2026-10-28). Docstring with estimand and represented sources; tests, including: for the same seed, the draws equal those of QuaPy's `sample_from_posterior` for both quantifiers.
  - `experiments/methods.py`: eight factory entries in the BayCAP pool (`BAYES_ENV`); `experiments/main.py`: for these methods, the existing columns `nuts_divergences`, `nuts_max_rhat`, `nuts_min_ess` and new optional per-bag columns (§V.3): `prev_mean` ($\bar\pi$), `acc_sd` ($\sigma_p$), `sigma_g` ($\bar\sigma_g$), `val_ece`.
  - `tables/`, `plots/`: a new E3.1 module each; `tables/displacement.py` with $R_0=0$ for fixed-$\hat C$ methods; labels in `tables/common.py` and `plots/common.py`; exclusion of the eight methods from the tables, ranks and default plots of `main` (`EXCLUDED_METHODS`).
  - `docs/work_plan.md`: §II.4; §II.12 (row of `BayCAPKDEy`: the class depends on more of QuaPy's private module `quapy.method._bayesian`, the models and their preparation, which matters for the move to `cap-ml`); §V.3 (new columns); FIX-3 (the `BayCAPKDEy` bullet is solved by the seeded MLP).
- **Depends on:** WP-4 (`realised_tables`, `BayesCAP-real`), WP-6 (`BayesCAP` on CPU from `BayCAP`, NUTS diagnostics in `main`, `tables/displacement.py --method`), WP-11 (the stored results of `BayesCAP-Hf-real`, done); solves part of FIX-3. No dependency on WP-7, but both touch `experiments/methods.py`.
- **Comparability:** adds eight methods to `main`; the stored results of every other method are untouched, and the paper's tables, ranks and default plots are unchanged (the eight are excluded). Until their results are stored, every pair of `main` counts as incomplete for `check_missing.py` (§V.1). `BayCAPKDEy` changes output (seeded MLP, possibly renamed); it has no stored results. Comparable with the CPU results of `BayesCAP`, `BayesCAP-real` and `BayesCAP-Hf-real` (same bags, seeds, platform).
- **Deviations from Part V:** the pilot uses LR and kNN instead of LR alone, because kNN's coarse outputs (multiples of $0.2$, exact zeros) are the case where the input classifier `h` can break either quantifier (user's agreement of 2026-10-28). Deviations from the card: the NUTS models of the two quantifiers are copied from QuaPy (commit `31f36f6`) instead of called as QuaPy's `_numpyro_model`, because QuaPy builds `BayesianKDEy` and `BayesianMAPLS` only when `pystan` is installed and `pystan` is not in the environment (user's decision of 2026-11-03); the equality with QuaPy's draws is tested by restoring, in the test only, the modules that QuaPy disables without `pystan`. The class is `methods/baycap_fixed.py:BayCAPFixed`, and `methods/baycap_kdey.py:BayCAPKDEy` is removed.
- **Done when:** the eight methods have stored results with diagnostics for all 75 pairs and the three metrics, and the E3.1 tables and plots are generated; the results are described in Part II with their location, the `cap-ml` commit and the date of the runs.
- **Commands:** all on `bcuda` (the default), each in its own tmux session; `make logs EXP=<target>` shows the output, `make explog EXP=main` the experiment log. The eight methods are `BayCAP-KDEy-h`, `BayCAP-KDEy-h-real`, `BayCAP-KDEy-mlp`, `BayCAP-KDEy-mlp-real`, `BayCAP-EMQ-h`, `BayCAP-EMQ-h-real`, `BayCAP-EMQ-mlp`, `BayCAP-EMQ-mlp-real`; they run in the BayCAP pool, on CPU (`BAYES_ENV`).
  - *Pilot:* `german` (binary) and `letter` ($K=26$) with LR and kNN, all eight methods: `make run-main-classic ARGS="--methods BayCAP-KDEy-h BayCAP-KDEy-h-real BayCAP-KDEy-mlp BayCAP-KDEy-mlp-real BayCAP-EMQ-h BayCAP-EMQ-h-real BayCAP-EMQ-mlp BayCAP-EMQ-mlp-real --datasets german letter --classifiers LR kNN"` ($4$ pairs × $8$ methods × $100$ bags $=3\,200$ NUTS runs); the times per bag are in `t_test_ave`.
  - *Full:* one method per run, one run at a time (each uses the whole BayCAP pool): `make run-main-classic ARGS="--methods <method>"` for each of the eight ($75\times100=7\,500$ NUTS runs each); cells already stored, e.g. by the pilot, are skipped.
  - *Tables:* `make run-tables-baycap-fixed ARGS=--tables` (needs the stored results of `BayesCAP`, `BayesCAP-real`, `BayesCAP-Hf-real` and `RQBS`; it works on the pairs stored for all twelve methods, so also after the pilot), then `make pull` and `make run-tables-baycap-fixed WHERE=local ARGS=--pdf` where `pdflatex` is installed. Decomposition of E2.2: `make run-tables-displacement ARGS="--tables --method <method>"` for each of the eight. Rank table with the eight: `make run-tables-rank ARGS="--tables --include-e31"` (output under `<OUT_DIR>/uncawe/tables/rank/extended/`).
  - *Plots:* `make run-plot-baycap-fixed` (reads the CSVs of the tables; `ARGS="--figures width"` for one figure), then `make pull`.
- **Log:** 2026-10-28: card added by the user. 2026-11-03: implementation started on branch `wp-8/fixed-c`. Check before coding: $L$ and $V$ are the two halves of a stratified split of the same training data (`cap.data.datasets.split_train`, `LabelledCollection.split_stratified(0.5, random_state=0)`), so $\max_j\lvert\pi^L_j-\pi^V_j\rvert$ is only rounding of the class counts, of order $1/\lvert V\rvert$, and QuaPy's source prevalence $\pi^P=\pi^V$ is kept for `EMQ` with `h`; the values per dataset were not computed (no code run). QuaPy's `quapy.method._bayesian` disables its Bayesian methods when `import stan` fails, and `pystan` is in no dependency of the project (QuaPy's `bayes` extra, `cap-ml`'s optional `stan` extra), so the user chose to copy the two models (see Deviations). `BayCAPFixed` in `methods/baycap_fixed.py` (§II.4), the eight methods in `experiments/methods.py:FIXED_C_VARIANTS`; `main` routes the methods of `BAYES_POOL_METHODS` to the BayCAP pool (before: every name containing `BayesCAP`; the new names do not) and stores the diagnostics and summaries of `BayCAPFixed` per bag (§V.3). The cross-fitting of the MLP runs with `n_jobs=1`, as QuaPy's default `qp.environ["N_JOBS"]=1`, so that a worker of the BayCAP pool keeps one core busy. New modules `tables/baycap_fixed.py` and `plots/baycap_fixed.py`, `make` targets `tables-baycap-fixed`, `plot-baycap-fixed`; `tables/displacement.py` with $R_0=0$ for the eight; `tables/rank.py --include-e31`. Implementation choices (not in the card): the observed widening factor of a pair is the median over the bags of the ratio of the realised to the population amplitude, and the predicted one the median over the bags of $\sqrt{1+(\bar\sigma_g/\sigma_p)^2}$, since $\sigma_p$ can be $0$ when the recalls are equal (§T.5); the mismatch uses the predicted-class shares of `true_cts` (its predicted margins, which carry no label) with $\hat C$ rebuilt from the validation counts and the stored `prev_mean`; the diagnostics and the summaries of a cell are read from its realised method, since the two methods of a cell share their draws; the bins of $\min_j\pi^Q_j$ are $[0,0.01)$, $[0.01,0.02)$, $[0.02,0.05)$, $[0.05,0.1)$, $[0.1,0.2)$, $[0.2,1)$; the mean Winkler rank of the $2\times2$ summary ranks the four cells of the same pushforward on each pair; the calibration scatter is coloured by $K$, with the classifier as marker; the bootstrap generator of each test depends on the seed, the pair and the test; the summary counts the pairs whose largest split-$\hat R$ exceeds $1.1$, the threshold of E2.8 (`tables/baycap_hf.py:RHAT_HIGH`; user's decision of 2026-11-03). Tests in `tests/test_baycap_fixed.py`, `tests/test_baycap_fixed_tables.py`, `tests/test_methods.py`. Not run. 2026-11-04: `devel` merged into the branch (four commits on the documents of Ext. 4, no conflict). User's decision: the realised methods are also compared with `BayesCAP-Hf-real`, the best BayCAP prior of E2.8 (stored full run, no new run), next to `BayesCAP-real`, `BayesCAP` and `RQBS`, which stay: its columns in the realised table, four more Winkler tests (realised method of each cell minus `BayesCAP-Hf-real`, $16$ in all, placed last so that the generators of the other twelve are unchanged), and a `coverage_hf` scatter. The pairs where NUTS is unreliable for BayCAP-Hf in E2.8 are reported with no flag (user's decision). 2026-11-05: after a run with a wrong method name (`BayesCAP-KDEy-h`, so no results and a pandas error), `tables/displacement.py --method` accepts only the BayCAP methods of `main` (`METHOD_CHOICES`: `BAYCAP_VARIANTS` and `FIXED_C_VARIANTS`, the methods whose prior on $C$ `method_c_prior` knows), at the user's request; a wrong name now stops with the list of valid ones. 2026-11-05: the `width` and `centre` figures failed to draw, since matplotlib's mathtext has no `\lVert`/`\rVert` (the smoke test built the figures without drawing them); the mismatch label now uses `\Vert`, and the test draws every figure. 2026-11-05: the PDF of the `design` table failed (`pdflatex`: the raw metric name `vanilla_accuracy`, an underscore outside math); its rows now show `vanilla accuracy` (`metric_label`), and the column of the MLP reads $s=$ mlp. 2026-11-05: full run by the user on `bcuda` (the eight methods, all $75$ pairs and three metrics, $100$ bags each; dates and commits of the runs not recorded here); the E3.1 tables, the E2.2 decompositions of the eight methods and the figures `coverage`, `coverage_population`, `coverage_hf`, `width` generated and copied locally. The figures `centre`, `calibration`, `min_prev` were not generated. The rank table with `--include-e31` ranked the sixteen methods but showed only the columns of `main`, because `tables/rank.py:_rank_latex_table` rebuilt its columns from `_methods()` without the included methods; it now takes the methods of `gen_tables`, stops if a ranked method has no column, and places the `\cmidrule` under each metric from the number of methods (before: fixed at columns $2$–$7$ and $8$–$13$, right only for six methods, so the rules of the rank table of `main` change too, not its numbers); tests in `tests/test_rank.py`. To generate again: the rank table (`make run-tables-rank ARGS=--include-e31`) and the three figures (`make run-plot-baycap-fixed ARGS="--figures centre calibration min_prev"`). Analysis written in [[ext3_posterior_propagation]] (E3.1), without ranks. 2026-11-05: at the user's request, the rank tables of `main` rank `BayesCAP-Hf-real` and no longer `BayesCAP-EB-real` and `BayesCAP-H-real` (`tables/rank.py`, §V.4); the main tables are unchanged. Not in the card's scope, kept on this branch because the E3.1 rank table needs it. To generate again: `make run-tables-rank` and `make run-tables-rank ARGS=--include-e31`. 2026-11-05: at the user's request, the realised and population tables of E3.1 group their columns by score (AE added, then amplitude, coverage, Winkler score) and then by method, with a colour scale and the best value in bold in each row and score group (coverage: closest to $0.95$); the standalone documents load `xcolor`. Tables to generate again: `make run-tables-baycap-fixed ARGS=--tables`, then `make pull` and `make run-tables-baycap-fixed WHERE=local ARGS=--pdf`. 2026-11-05: the rank table with `--include-e31` and the figures `centre`, `calibration`, `min_prev` generated by the user; the analysis of E3.1 completed with the ranks, the comparison of the two input classifiers of BayesKDEy and the conclusion (BayCAP-KDEy with the realised pushforward is the best of the eight methods; only the realised methods are considered from now on, user's decision); findings in §II.4. Card closed at the user's request.
- **Outcome:** merged into `devel` on 2026-11-05 (`1fc666d`), at the user's request; `devel` had no new commits, so no rebase. Deviations from the card are in its `Deviations` field (the NUTS models copied from QuaPy, as `pystan` is missing). Decisions beyond the card, in the log: the comparison with `BayesCAP-Hf-real`; the method names checked by `tables/displacement.py`; the score tables grouped by score and coloured per row, typeset on A3. Changes outside the card's scope, kept on the branch: the fix of the columns of `tables/rank.py` and the rank set of `main` with `BayesCAP-Hf-real` instead of `BayesCAP-EB-real` and `BayesCAP-H-real` (§V.4). Problems: the first rank table with the eight methods showed only the columns of `main` (fixed); three figures failed to draw (mathtext, fixed); the tests table of E3.1 still overflows its A4 page by about $107$pt. The dates and commits of the runs were not recorded. Unit tests: 230, all passing at the merge.
